Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The biochemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 632. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 62 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the biochemistry decomposition. By minimality, the element a^632 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 62-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
